| Amazon Web Services | hosted | documented | AWS-managed Code Interpreter built-in tool, using a system resource or a custom tool configuration; AWS Paid plan required | The AWS Free Tier page labels Amazon Bedrock AgentCore Paid plan exclusive; the AWS Free plan does not grant access to this managed tool even with a credit balance. Paid plan is an account access gate, not a required recurring Code Interpreter subscription; usage beyond eligible credits is billed. Also requires configured credentials, Code Interpreter IAM permissions and a supported region. aws.codeinterpreter.v1 is the default system tool; custom configurations change network, execution-role, certificate and mount settings, not the compute deployment model. Direct tool execution does not require a Bedrock model; model access is a prerequisite of the agent tutorial. Beta/experimental Outbound Policy injects named Secret headers outside the guest for HTTPS port 443 HTTP/1.1 only, up to 25 header values; no ephemeral Secrets, HTTP/2/gRPC or ECH injection. It cannot combine with block_network, CIDR/domain allowlists, sidecar relaying or memory snapshots; main container only, values refresh on creation/policy update rather than automatic Secret rotation. | Tensorlake | volumes | documented | Separate durable versioned POSIX filesystem mounts; Git workspace mounts have different publication semantics | Create filesystem before attaching; up to 8 absolute non-nested mounts per sandbox. Disjoint paths merge, same-path writes last-writer-wins. Server autosave WAL retains the newest 256 generations and all generations from the last 24 hours; permanent snapshots are separately billed until deleted. After a crash, remounting on another machine recovers through the last acknowledged server autosave, not unreplicated tail writes; on the same machine, its overlay/local journal can also recover unsaved changes. Current reviewed session/introduction docs do not establish per-filesystem session or session-metadata caps, precise crash-detection or salvage intervals, or eager-GC timing, so those numeric/behavioral limits remain unverified rather than zero or unlimited. Pins require read-only permanent snapshots/latest fleet support; invalid runtime attach can terminate sandbox. Git private WAL (30 seconds) is not branch commit; reattach explicit workspace. | Vercel | outbound_restrictions | documented | Default allow-all; deny-all including DNS; custom default-deny domain/CIDR policies with live updates | Full firewall is available on Hobby too. Denied CIDRs override allows. Domain rules use TLS SNI (including TLS Postgres; GSSAPI unsupported), not plaintext HTTP or HTTP Host; domain fronting may reach other virtual hosts at an allowed endpoint. Allowed CIDRs bypass domain matching/brokering/proxying; broad ranges override domain scope and CIDR-only policies leave DNS unrestricted. Transform/forwardURL rules terminate TLS, but matchers only select handling, never block: unmatched requests still pass unchanged. Catch-all * permits domain-less traffic without brokering/forwarding. To pin Host or enforce paths, apply transform/forwardURL without match and enforce policy in the proxy. No per-port policy control established. Egress controls are separate from public ingress. | https://vercel.com/docs/sandbox/concepts/firewall https://vercel.com/changelog/full-sandbox-egress-firewall-now-available-on-hobby-plan |
